                    seen.add(l.target);queue.append(l.target)
        return target in seen
    if reachable(lambda w:w is True):return True
    if reachable(lambda w:w is not False):return None
    return False if closed else None
def run(x):
    check(x)
    eps=exact(x.epsilon)
    state={s:exact(v) for s,v in zip(x.sites,x.initial)}
    # For each site, certified external history must be identified as event, not inferred from nonzero state.
    formation=defaultdict(list); history_snapshots=defaultdict(list)
    signal_marks=defaultdict(list)  # 界外成因：已认证、有效的界外作用分项（脉冲或界外载体过程）在该位留下的改变
    part=[];hdyn=[];reports=[]
    for t in range(x.window[0]-1,x.window[-1]+1):
        before=dict(state); contributions=defaultdict(list);events=[]
        # Pulse first as an event, but processes read the prior snapshot, not pulse-modified value.
        for p in x.pulses:
            if p.step!=t:continue
            # 只计已认证发生且有效的脉冲；确认不发生者不计贡献、不登记形成与成因
            a=exact(p.amount)
            if p.certified is True and a>eps:
                contributions[p.target].append((p.name,a))
                formation[p.target].append((p.name,t,p.external))
                if p.external:signal_marks[p.target].append((p.name,t))
        for m in x.processes:
            if m.step!=t:continue
            k=exact(m.coefficient); b=exact(m.bias)
            if m.law=='constant':value=b
            elif m.law=='linear':value=b+k*sum(before[s] for s in m.reads)
            else:value=b+k*before[m.reads[0]]*before[m.reads[1]]
            is_action=tri_and(abs(value)>eps,m.certified_effect)
            # Declared reads do not establish a genuine dependence: compute the law's support.
            support=()
            # 组织条件的支持：数值律依赖读取位，或纯读取过程（分项为零）；读取认证另作合取，未定不预先截为假
            # 函数支持按数学上的非零偏导判定（系数与伙伴读数精确不为零），不与有效改变阈值 ε 混用
            if m.law=='linear' and (k!=0 or abs(value)<=eps): support=m.reads
            elif m.law=='bilinear' and k!=0:
                support=tuple(s for i,s in enumerate(m.reads) if before[m.reads[1-i]]!=0)
            # For every element in the declared T, historical formation must precede the read.
            active_T=set(x.declared_T)
            available=[s for s in support if s in active_T and before[s]>eps and any(a[1]<t for a in formation[s])]
            is_cond=tri_and(bool(available),m.certified_read)
            # No automatic path from a read-site to an act. Explicit event links are required.
            old_nodes=[f'state:{s}:{t}' for s in available]
            route=path_status(x.links,old_nodes,m.name,x.closed_graph) if old_nodes else False
            # Action provenance must be supported by a nonzero functional dependence,
            # not merely a certified read that has no effect on the action.
            action_support=()
            if m.law=='linear' and k!=0:action_support=m.reads
            elif m.law=='bilinear' and k!=0:
                action_support=tuple(s for i,s in enumerate(m.reads) if before[m.reads[1-i]]!=0)
            action_available=[s for s in action_support if s in active_T and before[s]>eps and any(a[1]<t for a in formation[s])]
            action_nodes=[f'state:{s}:{t}' for s in action_available]
            action_route=path_status(x.links,action_nodes,m.name,x.closed_graph) if action_nodes else False
            is_hist=tri_and(bool(action_available),m.certified_read,action_route)
            if is_action is True:
                contributions[m.target].append((m.name,value))
                # 新元素的形成：B 内载体者为场内续行形成，界外载体者为信号形成
                if value>eps and before[m.target]<=eps:
                    formation[m.target].append((m.name,t,not m.carrier_inside))
                # 界外载体过程的有效分项，不论新旧元素、不论正负，都是信号留下的改变
                if not m.carrier_inside:
                    signal_marks[m.target].append((m.name,t))
            elif is_action is None: # 防御性检查：作用认证未定已由 check() 排除于定义域之外
                raise ValueError('unresolved effect cannot determine numeric trajectory')
            if t in x.window:
                if m.carrier_inside:part.append(is_cond)
                hdyn.append(tri_and(m.target in active_T,is_action,is_hist))
            events.append({'id':m.name,'delta':value,'act':is_action,'cond':is_cond,'histcond':is_hist,'route':route})
        for s in x.sites:
            new=before[s]+sum(v for _,v in contributions[s])
            # 深度不得为负：精确更新值小于零者越出定义域，不截零、不设容差
            if new < 0:raise ValueError('negative trace depth')
            state[s]=new
            if state[s]>eps and formation[s]:history_snapshots[s].append(t+1)
        if t in x.window:reports.append({'step':t,'before':before,'after':dict(state),'contributions':dict(contributions),'events':events})
    ts=x.declared_T
    eligible=all(state[s]>eps and formation[s] for s in ts)
    sources={origin for site in ts for origin,t in signal_marks[site]}
    trace=tri_and(bool(sources),eligible,x.identity,x.trace_history)
    pw=tri_or(part); hw=tri_or(hdyn)
    return {'trace':trace,'part':pw,'hdyn':hw,'Q':tri_and(trace,pw,hw),'reports':reports,'physical_certificate':False}
~~~

A.4　snn\_core.py　上游冻结程序：《候选宿载场具化判定式》v1.0 附录 A.1（冻结 SNN 实例 s1，即该篇实例 P 的冻结程序）

~~~python
"""冻结 SNN 实例 s1：SNN 可重放实例（程序宿载）。精确有理运算，Python 标准库，确定性，可逐事件重放。
宿载实例 χ_SNN = (P, L, B, W)：
  P 本程序；L 算法层（离散时间步）；B = {4 个输入神经元, 2 个输出 LIF 神经元, 8 个突触}；W 申报窗。
  输入神经元的发放由外部刺激序列决定（界外信号）；教学电流 teach 只在形成期注入输出 0（界外信号）。
  输出神经元：LIF，v ← v·LEAK + Σ w_ij·s_i + I_ext；v ≥ θ 时发放并复位为 0。
  可塑性：成对 STDP（迹式）：前突触迹 x_i、后突触迹 y_j 每步乘 DEC，发放时加 1；
    后神经元 j 发放时（经反传信号到达突触）w_ij += A_PLUS·x_i；前神经元 i 发放时 w_ij -= A_MINUS·y_j；
    权重夹在 [0, W_MAX]。另有可选的向基线弛豫（RELAX，默认 0）。
  干预开关：plastic（可塑性开/关）；bap（后神经元发放是否传回突触——关闭时 STDP 看不到后发放，即切断途径）。
迹应：超基线权重 z_ij = w_ij − W0。
"""
from fractions import Fraction as F
import hashlib
N_IN, N_OUT = 4, 2
W0 = F(1, 4)
LEAK, THETA, DEC = F(3, 4), F(1), F(1, 2)
A_PLUS, A_MINUS, W_MAX = F(1, 8), F(1, 16), F(2)
PAT = {'A': (1, 1, 0, 0), 'B': (0, 0, 1, 1), 'AB': (1, 1, 1, 1), 'none': (0, 0, 0, 0)}

def init(w=None):
    return dict(w=[[W0 if w is None else w[i][j] for j in range(N_OUT)] for i in range(N_IN)],
                v=[F(0)] * N_OUT, x=[F(0)] * N_IN, y=[F(0)] * N_OUT)

def step(st, inp, teach=(0, 0), plastic=True, bap=True, relax=F(0)):
    """一步：返回 (新状态, 事件记录)。事件记录列出前发放、后发放与每个突触的可塑性分项（正、负、弛豫）。"""
    w = [row[:] for row in st['w']]
    s_in = PAT[inp]
    v = [st['v'][j] * LEAK + sum(w[i][j] * s_in[i] for i in range(N_IN)) + teach[j] for j in range(N_OUT)]
    s_out = [1 if v[j] >= THETA else 0 for j in range(N_OUT)]
    v = [F(0) if s_out[j] else v[j] for j in range(N_OUT)]
    x = [st['x'][i] * DEC + s_in[i] for i in range(N_IN)]
    y = [st['y'][j] * DEC + s_out[j] for j in range(N_OUT)]
    comp = {}
    for i in range(N_IN):
        for j in range(N_OUT):
            pos = A_PLUS * x[i] if (plastic and bap and s_out[j]) else F(0)
            neg = -A_MINUS * y[j] if (plastic and s_in[i] and bap) else F(0)
            rel = -relax * (w[i][j] - W0) if plastic else F(0)
            nw = min(W_MAX, max(F(0), w[i][j] + pos + neg + rel))
            comp[(i, j)] = (pos, neg, rel, nw - w[i][j])
            w[i][j] = nw
    ev = dict(inp=inp, teach=teach, s_in=s_in, s_out=tuple(s_out), comp=comp)
    return dict(w=w, v=v, x=x, y=y), ev

def run(hist, st=None, **kw):
    """hist：[(输入名, 教学电流)]。返回 (末态, 事件日志)。"""
    st = st or init()
    log = []
    for inp, teach in hist:
        st, ev = step(st, inp, teach, **kw)
        log.append(ev)
    return st, log

def log_hash(log):
    """冻结日志的指纹：事件按确定顺序序列化后取 SHA-256。"""
    h = hashlib.sha256()
    for ev in log:
        h.update(repr((ev['inp'], ev['teach'], ev['s_in'], ev['s_out'],
                       sorted((k, tuple(str(c) for c in v)) for k, v in ev['comp'].items()))).encode())
    return h.hexdigest()

def z(st):
    return [[st['w'][i][j] - W0 for j in range(N_OUT)] for i in range(N_IN)]
~~~

A.5　ann20f\_models.py　本篇的库：SNN s1/s2、梯度训练网络及其变体、KV 缓存与快权重的申报模型

~~~python
"""《人工神经网络迹应变换区分式》v1.0 附录程序：申报模型库。只用 Python 标准库；一切数值为 fractions.Fraction；无随机数
（随机史由 run20.py 以固定种子生成，只作有限核验的取样）。
一、SNN（程序宿载，局部可塑）
  snn_beat       s1 参数下与冻结 snn_core.step 同一按拍顺序的独立实现；另可开 s2 扩展：字母 A2（梯度网络另用 C）、同拍竞争（WTA）、
                 发放后全体复位（GRESET）、弛豫 RELAX。s1 = 全部扩展关闭；s1 的判词不受 s2 改写。
  SNNJH          〔十七〕附录 A.1 jhc_core 的宿载接口：X 含 8 个权重位与 v、x、y（活动与残留同列为载体，以便三分）。
  snn_jxs        〔十八〕附录 A.1 jxs_core 的宿载接口：d = w − W0（8 位），r = (v, x, y)。
二、梯度训练网络（同构 4→2 线性单元，在线 delta 规则 / 平方误差 SGD）
  GDJH, gd_jxs   写入分解为两过程（〔申〕）：TGT = η·s_i·t_j（外标项，不读迹应）与 ERR = −η·s_i·y_j（误差自项，读迹应）。
  GDAdaJH        律层变体：每位累积量 acc（有理 AdaGrad 型：步长 η/(1 + acc)），历史控律层分量（〔十八〕D18-11）。
  GDClockJH      钟控变体：步长按拍钟 clk 在第 K 拍后减半（钟控律层）。
  SelfTrainJH    自参照目标变体：t_j = [y_j ≥ 1/2]（伪标签），目标由宿载自身活动生成。
三、推理期状态载体
  FWHebbJH       外积快权重（线性注意）：S ← λS + v kᵀ；写入不读 S，退浅直读 S。
  FWDeltaJH      delta 规则快权重：S ← S + β(v − S k)kᵀ；写入经检索活动 S k 读 S。
  KVJH           两层只增 KV 缓存（标量键值，非负分数归一化注意；第一层写入只读当拍输入，第二层写入读第一层注意输出）。
参数一律为构造值（无量纲有理数），不是测量值。
"""
from fractions import Fraction as F
import jhc_core as JH
import jxs_core as JX
ZERO = F(0)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------- SNN
PAT = {'A': (1, 1, 0, 0), 'B': (0, 0, 1, 1), 'AB': (1, 1, 1, 1), 'none': (0, 0, 0, 0), 'A2': (0, 1, 1, 0), 'C': (1, 0, 0, 0), 'I1': (0, 1, 0, 0), 'I2': (0, 0, 1, 0)}
S1 = dict(N_IN=4, N_OUT=2, W0=F(1, 4), LEAK=F(3, 4), THETA=F(1), DEC=F(1, 2), A_PLUS=F(1, 8), A_MINUS=F(1, 16),
          W_MAX=F(2), RELAX=F(0), WTA=False, GRESET=False)

def params(**kw):
    p = dict(S1)
    p.update(kw)
    return p

def parse(sig):
    """字母：'A'、'B'、'AB'、'A2'、'rest'（= none）；后缀 '+T0'/'+T1' 表示向输出 0/1 注入教学电流 1（界外信号）。"""
    teach = [ZERO, ZERO]
    name = sig
    if '+T' in sig:
        name, j = sig.split('+T')
        teach[int(j)] = F(1)
    if name == 'rest':
        name = 'none'
    return PAT[name], tuple(teach)

def snn_init(p, w=None):
    n, m = p['N_IN'], p['N_OUT']
    return dict(w={(i, j): (p['W0'] if w is None else w[(i, j)]) for i in range(n) for j in range(m)},
                v=[ZERO] * m, x=[ZERO] * n, y=[ZERO] * m)

def snn_gate(p, vt):
    m = len(vt)
    g = [1 if vt[j] >= p['THETA'] else 0 for j in range(m)]
    if p['WTA'] and sum(g) > 1:
        top = max(vt[j] for j in range(m) if g[j])
        g = [1 if (g[j] and vt[j] == top) else 0 for j in range(m)]
    return g

def snn_beat(p, st, sig, plastic=True, bap=True):
    """一拍。执行顺序同 snn_core.step（积分读拍前 w → 门控 → 复位 → 迹更新 → 增强、抑制、弛豫 → 夹限）。"""
    n, m = p['N_IN'], p['N_OUT']
    s_in, teach = parse(sig)
    w = dict(st['w'])
    vt = [st['v'][j] * p['LEAK'] + sum(w[(i, j)] * s_in[i] for i in range(n)) + teach[j] for j in range(m)]
    g = snn_gate(p, vt)
    if p['GRESET'] and any(g):
        v = [ZERO] * m
    else:
        v = [ZERO if g[j] else vt[j] for j in range(m)]
    x = [st['x'][i] * p['DEC'] + s_in[i] for i in range(n)]
    y = [st['y'][j] * p['DEC'] + g[j] for j in range(m)]
    comp = {}
    for i in range(n):
        for j in range(m):
            pos = p['A_PLUS'] * x[i] if (plastic and bap and g[j]) else ZERO
            neg = -p['A_MINUS'] * y[j] if (plastic and s_in[i] and bap) else ZERO
            rel = -p['RELAX'] * (w[(i, j)] - p['W0']) if plastic else ZERO
            nw = min(p['W_MAX'], max(ZERO, w[(i, j)] + pos + neg + rel))
            comp[(i, j)] = (pos, neg, rel, nw - w[(i, j)])
            w[(i, j)] = nw
    return dict(w=w, v=v, x=x, y=y), dict(sig=sig, s_in=s_in, g=tuple(g), vt=tuple(vt), comp=comp)

def snn_run(p, hist, st=None, **kw):
    st = st or snn_init(p)
    log, sts = [], [st]
    for s in hist:
        st, ev = snn_beat(p, st, s, **kw)
        log.append(ev)
        sts.append(st)
    return sts, log

def z_of(p, st, ij):
    return st['w'][ij] - p['W0']

def wname(i, j):
    return 'w%d%d' % (i, j)

class SNNJH(JH.Host):
    """〔十七〕接口：X = 权重 8 位 + v0、v1 + x0..x3 + y0、y1。活动 y（act）：vt、g、新迹 xn、yn。
    过程：PLUS、MINUS、RELAX 作用于权重；LIF 作用于 v；XTR、YTR 作用于迹。"""
    name = 'SNN'
    grid = None
    def __init__(self, p, plastic=True, bap=True):
        self.p, self.plastic, self.bap = p, plastic, bap
        n, m = p['N_IN'], p['N_OUT']
        self.W = [wname(i, j) for i in range(n) for j in range(m)]
        self.X = self.W + ['v%d' % j for j in range(m)] + ['x%d' % i for i in range(n)] + ['y%d' % j for j in range(m)]
        big = F(10 ** 6)
        self.lo = {x: (ZERO if x[0] in 'wxy' else -big) for x in self.X}
        self.hi = {x: (p['W_MAX'] if x[0] == 'w' else big) for x in self.X}
        self.ground = {x: (p['W0'] if x[0] == 'w' else ZERO) for x in self.X}
        self.orient = {x: 1 for x in self.X}
        self.init = dict(self.ground)
    def to_st(self, w):
        p = self.p
        n, m = p['N_IN'], p['N_OUT']
        return dict(w={(i, j): w[wname(i, j)] for i in range(n) for j in range(m)},
                    v=[w['v%d' % j] for j in range(m)], x=[w['x%d' % i] for i in range(n)], y=[w['y%d' % j] for j in range(m)])
    def from_st(self, st):
        out = {wname(i, j): st['w'][(i, j)] for (i, j) in st['w']}
        out.update({'v%d' % j: st['v'][j] for j in range(len(st['v']))})
        out.update({'x%d' % i: st['x'][i] for i in range(len(st['x']))})
        out.update({'y%d' % j: st['y'][j] for j in range(len(st['y']))})
        return out
    def act(self, w, s):
        p = self.p
        n, m = p['N_IN'], p['N_OUT']
        s_in, teach = parse(s)
        vt = [w['v%d' % j] * p['LEAK'] + sum(w[wname(i, j)] * s_in[i] for i in range(n)) + teach[j] for j in range(m)]
        g = snn_gate(p, vt)
        y = {}
        for j in range(m):
            y['vt%d' % j] = vt[j]
            y['g%d' % j] = F(g[j])
            y['yn%d' % j] = w['y%d' % j] * p['DEC'] + g[j]
        for i in range(n):
            y['xn%d' % i] = w['x%d' % i] * p['DEC'] + s_in[i]
        return y
    def comps(self, w, y, s):
        p = self.p
        n, m = p['N_IN'], p['N_OUT']
        s_in, _ = parse(s)
        out = []
        anyg = any(y['g%d' % j] for j in range(m))
        for j in range(m):
            g = y['g%d' % j]
            newv = ZERO if (g or (p['GRESET'] and anyg)) else y['vt%d' % j]
            out.append(('LIF', 'v%d' % j, newv - w['v%d' % j]))
            out.append(('YTR', 'y%d' % j, y['yn%d' % j] - w['y%d' % j]))
        for i in range(n):
            out.append(('XTR', 'x%d' % i, y['xn%d' % i] - w['x%d' % i]))
            for j in range(m):
                x = wname(i, j)
                pos = p['A_PLUS'] * y['xn%d' % i] if (self.plastic and self.bap and y['g%d' % j]) else ZERO
                neg = -p['A_MINUS'] * y['yn%d' % j] if (self.plastic and s_in[i] and self.bap) else ZERO
                rel = -p['RELAX'] * (w[x] - p['W0']) if self.plastic else ZERO
                out += [('PLUS', x, pos), ('MINUS', x, neg), ('RELAX', x, rel)]
        return out

def snn_jxs(p, plastic=True):
    """〔十八〕接口：d = w − W0；r = (v, x, y)；post = 逐位夹限（LP）。"""
    n, m = p['N_IN'], p['N_OUT']
    pos_ = [wname(i, j) for i in range(n) for j in range(m)]
    def read(d, r, s):
        s_in, teach = parse(s)
        vt = [r['v'][j] * p['LEAK'] + sum((p['W0'] + d[wname(i, j)]) * s_in[i] for i in range(n)) + teach[j] for j in range(m)]
        g = snn_gate(p, vt)
        y = {'vt%d' % j: vt[j] for j in range(m)}
        y.update({'g%d' % j: F(g[j]) for j in range(m)})
        y.update({'xn%d' % i: r['x'][i] * p['DEC'] + s_in[i] for i in range(n)})
        y.update({'yn%d' % j: r['y'][j] * p['DEC'] + g[j] for j in range(m)})
        y['s_in'] = s_in
        return y
    def comps(d, y, s):
        out = {}
        for i in range(n):
            for j in range(m):
                pos = p['A_PLUS'] * y['xn%d' % i] if (plastic and y['g%d' % j]) else ZERO
                neg = -p['A_MINUS'] * y['yn%d' % j] if (plastic and y['s_in'][i]) else ZERO
                rel = -p['RELAX'] * d[wname(i, j)] if plastic else ZERO
                out[wname(i, j)] = [('PLUS', pos), ('MINUS', neg), ('RELAX', rel)]
        return out
    def post(dn, do):
        return {x: min(p['W_MAX'] - p['W0'], max(-p['W0'], v)) for x, v in dn.items()}
    def resid(r, y):
        anyg = any(y['g%d' % j] for j in range(m))
        v = [ZERO if (y['g%d' % j] or (p['GRESET'] and anyg)) else y['vt%d' % j] for j in range(m)]
        return dict(v=v, x=[y['xn%d' % i] for i in range(n)], y=[y['yn%d' % j] for j in range(m)])
    return JX.Host(pos_, read, comps, post, resid)

def snn_r0(p):
    return dict(v=[ZERO] * p['N_OUT'], x=[ZERO] * p['N_IN'], y=[ZERO] * p['N_OUT'])

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- 梯度训练网络
GD = dict(N_IN=4, N_OUT=2, W0=F(1, 4), ETA=F(1, 4))

def gparse(sig):
    """字母：'A'、'B'、'A2'、'AB'、'rest'；后缀 ':t10' 等表示目标 (t0, t1)（界外教学信号）；无后缀者无目标、无写入。"""
    if ':t' in sig:
        name, t = sig.split(':t')
        tgt = (F(int(t[0])), F(int(t[1])))
    else:
        name, tgt = sig, None
    if name == 'rest':
        name = 'none'
    return PAT[name], tgt

class GDJH(JH.Host):
    """线性 4→2，y_j = Σ_i w_ij s_i；有目标的拍写入 TGT_ij = η s_i t_j、ERR_ij = −η s_i y_j（二者和即平方误差 SGD 一步）。
    inside：优化器是否申报在 B 内（只影响场型登记，不影响运行）。"""
    name = 'GD'
    grid = None
    def __init__(self, g=GD, eta=None, inside=True):
        self.g = dict(g)
        if eta is not None:
            self.g['ETA'] = eta
        self.inside = inside
        n, m = self.g['N_IN'], self.g['N_OUT']
        self.X = [wname(i, j) for i in range(n) for j in range(m)]
        self.lo = {x: F(-10 ** 6) for x in self.X}
        self.hi = {x: F(10 ** 6) for x in self.X}
        self.ground = {x: self.g['W0'] for x in self.X}
        self.orient = {x: 1 for x in self.X}
        self.init = dict(self.ground)
    def act(self, w, s):
        n, m = self.g['N_IN'], self.g['N_OUT']
        s_in, tgt = gparse(s)
        y = {'y%d' % j: sum(w[wname(i, j)] * s_in[i] for i in range(n)) for j in range(m)}
        return y
    def rate(self, w, i, j):
        return self.g['ETA']
    def comps(self, w, y, s):
        n, m = self.g['N_IN'], self.g['N_OUT']
        s_in, tgt = gparse(s)
        out = []
        if tgt is None:
            return out
        for i in range(n):
            for j in range(m):
                eta = self.rate(w, i, j)
                out.append(('TGT', wname(i, j), eta * s_in[i] * tgt[j]))
                out.append(('ERR', wname(i, j), -eta * s_in[i] * y['y%d' % j]))
        return out

class GDAdaJH(GDJH):
    """历史控律层：X 另含 acc_ij；步长 η/(1 + acc_ij)；有目标的拍 acc_ij += g²（g = s_i(t_j − y_j)）。Φ 不读 acc（隐律层）。"""
    name = 'GDAda'
    def __init__(self, g=GD, eta=None):
        super().__init__(g, eta)
        acc = ['a%d%d' % (i, j) for i in range(self.g['N_IN']) for j in range(self.g['N_OUT'])]
        self.X = self.X + acc
        for a in acc:
            self.lo[a], self.hi[a], self.ground[a], self.orient[a] = ZERO, F(10 ** 6), ZERO, 1
        self.init = dict(self.ground)
    def rate(self, w, i, j):
        return self.g['ETA'] / (1 + w['a%d%d' % (i, j)])
    def comps(self, w, y, s):
        out = super().comps(w, y, s)
        s_in, tgt = gparse(s)
        if tgt is None:
            return out
        for i in range(self.g['N_IN']):
            for j in range(self.g['N_OUT']):
                gr = s_in[i] * (tgt[j] - y['y%d' % j])
                out.append(('ACC', 'a%d%d' % (i, j), gr * gr))
        return out

class GDClockJH(GDJH):
    """钟控律层：X 另含拍钟 clk（每拍 +1，不读迹应与活动）；clk ≥ K 时步长减半。"""
    name = 'GDClock'
    def __init__(self, K, g=GD, eta=None):
        super().__init__(g, eta)
        self.K = K
        self.X = self.X + ['clk']
        self.lo['clk'], self.hi['clk'], self.ground['clk'], self.orient['clk'] = ZERO, F(10 ** 6), ZERO, 1
        self.init = dict(self.ground)
    def rate(self, w, i, j):
        return self.g['ETA'] if w['clk'] < self.K else self.g['ETA'] / 2
    def comps(self, w, y, s):
        return super().comps(w, y, s) + [('CLK', 'clk', F(1))]

class SelfTrainJH(GDJH):
    """自参照目标：无界外目标的拍以 t_j = [y_j ≥ 1/2] 为目标写入（目标由宿载自身活动生成）。"""
    name = 'SelfTrain'
    def comps(self, w, y, s):
        n, m = self.g['N_IN'], self.g['N_OUT']
        s_in, tgt = gparse(s)
        if tgt is not None:
            return super().comps(w, y, s)
        if sum(s_in) == 0:
            return []
        out = []
        for i in range(n):
            for j in range(m):
                t = F(1) if y['y%d' % j] >= F(1, 2) else ZERO
                out.append(('PSEUDO', wname(i, j), self.g['ETA'] * s_in[i] * (t - y['y%d' % j])))
        return out

def gd_jxs(g=GD):
    n, m = g['N_IN'], g['N_OUT']
    pos_ = [wname(i, j) for i in range(n) for j in range(m)]
    def read(d, r, s):
        s_in, tgt = gparse(s)
        y = {'y%d' % j: sum((g['W0'] + d[wname(i, j)]) * s_in[i] for i in range(n)) for j in range(m)}
        y['s_in'], y['tgt'] = s_in, tgt
        return y
    def comps(d, y, s):
        out = {}
        if y['tgt'] is None:
            return out
        for i in range(n):
            for j in range(m):
                out[wname(i, j)] = [('TGT', g['ETA'] * y['s_in'][i] * y['tgt'][j]),
                                    ('ERR', -g['ETA'] * y['s_in'][i] * y['y%d' % j])]
        return out
    return JX.Host(pos_, read, comps)

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- 推理期状态载体
TOK = {'a': ((F(1), F(0)), (F(1), F(0))), 'b': ((F(0), F(1)), (F(0), F(1))), 'c': ((F(1), F(1)), (F(0), F(1)))}

class FWBase(JH.Host):
    grid = None
    def __init__(self):
        self.X = ['S%d%d' % (a, b) for a in range(2) for b in range(2)]
        self.lo = {x: F(-10 ** 6) for x in self.X}
        self.hi = {x: F(10 ** 6) for x in self.X}
        self.ground = {x: ZERO for x in self.X}
        self.orient = {x: 1 for x in self.X}
        self.init = dict(self.ground)
    def kv(self, s):
        return TOK[s] if s in TOK else ((ZERO, ZERO), (ZERO, ZERO))
    def act(self, w, s):
        k, _v = self.kv(s)
        return {'r%d' % a: sum(w['S%d%d' % (a, b)] * k[b] for b in range(2)) for a in range(2)}

class FWHebbJH(FWBase):
    """S ← λS + v kᵀ：WRITE = v_a k_b（只读当拍输入），DECAY = −(1 − λ)S_ab（直读本位）。"""
    name = 'FWHebb'
    def __init__(self, lam=F(3, 4)):
        super().__init__()
        self.lam = lam
    def comps(self, w, y, s):
        k, v = self.kv(s)
        out = []
        for a in range(2):
            for b in range(2):
                x = 'S%d%d' % (a, b)
                out += [('WRITE', x, v[a] * k[b]), ('DECAY', x, -(1 - self.lam) * w[x])]
        return out

class FWDeltaJH(FWBase):
    """S ← S + β(v − S k)kᵀ：DELTA = β(v_a − r_a)k_b，r = S k 为检索活动。"""
    name = 'FWDelta'
    def __init__(self, beta=F(1, 2)):
        super().__init__()
        self.beta = beta
    def comps(self, w, y, s):
        k, v = self.kv(s)
        return [('DELTA', 'S%d%d' % (a, b), self.beta * (v[a] - y['r%d' % a]) * k[b]) for a in range(2) for b in range(2)]

EMB = {'p': F(1), 'q': F(2), 'u': F(3)}

class KVJH(JH.Host):
    """两层只增 KV 缓存，容量 N。X：n（计数，已写入条目数）、L1 键值 k1_i、v1_i，L2 键值 k2_i、v2_i（i < N）。
    分数 sc = 键 × 查询（均非负），注意输出 = Σ sc·值 / Σ sc（含当拍条目）。第一层：键 = 值 = 输入嵌入 e；
    第二层：键 = 值 = 第一层注意输出 o1（读第一层既有缓存）。layers = 1 时只有第一层。"""
    name = 'KV'
    grid = None
    def __init__(self, N=5, layers=2):
        self.N, self.layers = N, layers
        self.X = ['n'] + ['%s%d_%d' % (kv, l, i) for l in range(1, layers + 1) for kv in 'kv' for i in range(N)]
        self.lo = {x: ZERO for x in self.X}
        self.hi = {x: F(10 ** 6) for x in self.X}
        self.ground = {x: ZERO for x in self.X}
        self.orient = {x: 1 for x in self.X}
        self.init = dict(self.ground)
    @staticmethod
    def attend(keys, vals, q):
        sc = [k * q for k in keys]
        tot = sum(sc)
        return sum(a * v for a, v in zip(sc, vals)) / tot if tot else ZERO
    def act(self, w, s):
        n = int(w['n'])
        e = EMB.get(s)
        if e is None or n >= self.N:
            return {'o1': ZERO, 'o2': ZERO, 'on': F(0)}
        k1 = [w['k1_%d' % i] for i in range(n)] + [e]
        v1 = [w['v1_%d' % i] for i in range(n)] + [e]
        o1 = self.attend(k1, v1, e)
        y = {'o1': o1, 'on': F(1), 'o2': ZERO}
        if self.layers >= 2:
            k2 = [w['k2_%d' % i] for i in range(n)] + [o1]
            v2 = [w['v2_%d' % i] for i in range(n)] + [o1]
            y['o2'] = self.attend(k2, v2, o1)
        return y
    def comps(self, w, y, s):
        n = int(w['n'])
        e = EMB.get(s)
        if e is None or n >= self.N:
            return []
        out = [('CNT', 'n', F(1)), ('GEN1k', 'k1_%d' % n, e), ('GEN1v', 'v1_%d' % n, e)]
        if self.layers >= 2:
            out += [('GEN2k', 'k2_%d' % n, y['o1']), ('GEN2v', 'v2_%d' % n, y['o1'])]
        return out
    def readout(self, w, q, layer=1):
        n = int(w['n'])
        ks = [w['k%d_%d' % (layer, i)] for i in range(n)]
        vs = [w['v%d_%d' % (layer, i)] for i in range(n)]
        return self.attend(ks, vs, q)

class GD1JH(GDJH):
    """单过程申报（Δ_GD-1）：SGD_ij = η s_i (t_j − y_j) 作一个过程；与 GDJH（Δ_GD-2：TGT、ERR 两过程）同一运行。"""
    name = 'GD1'
    def comps(self, w, y, s):
        n, m = self.g['N_IN'], self.g['N_OUT']
        s_in, tgt = gparse(s)
        if tgt is None:
            return []
        return [('SGD', wname(i, j), self.rate(w, i, j) * s_in[i] * (tgt[j] - y['y%d' % j])) for i in range(n) for j in range(m)]

class GDStepClockJH(GDClockJH):
    """步钟：clk 只在执行优化步的拍（字母带目标后缀，含零梯度步）+1；空等拍不走。"""
    name = 'GDStepClock'
    def comps(self, w, y, s):
        out = GDJH.comps(self, w, y, s)
        _s_in, tgt = gparse(s)
        return out + ([('CLK', 'clk', F(1))] if tgt is not None else [])

class GD2JH(GDJH):
    """另立实现：算法层分别执行、可独立关闭并向共同后态贡献的两条通道——教学通道 TGT（读外标）与误差通道 ERR（读前向输出），
    各有独立开关 tgt_on、err_on，可逐项干预。两路都开时与 GD1JH 的运行逐拍相同。"""
    name = 'GD2'
    def __init__(self, g=GD, eta=None, tgt_on=True, err_on=True):
        super().__init__(g, eta)
        self.tgt_on, self.err_on = tgt_on, err_on
    def comps(self, w, y, s):
        out = super().comps(w, y, s)
        return [(p, x, c) for (p, x, c) in out if (p == 'TGT' and self.tgt_on) or (p == 'ERR' and self.err_on)]

class GDIdleClockJH(GDClockJH):
    """反例用：时钟只在不执行优化步的空等拍 +1（三个写入率候选之外的合法构造）。"""
    name = 'GDIdleClock'
    def comps(self, w, y, s):
        out = super().comps(w, y, s)
        if gparse(s)[1] is not None:
            return [c for c in out if c[0] != 'CLK']
        return out

每项输出「通过／失败」；末尾汇总；任一项失败时退出码为 1。只用标准库；一切数值为 Fraction；随机取样用固定种子。

import snn_core as SC          # 〔封十一〕附录 A.1（冻结 SNN 实例 s1）
import jhc_core as JH          # 〔十七〕附录 A.1
import jxs_core as JX          # 〔十八〕附录 A.1

def sha_file(p):
    return hashlib.sha256(open(p, 'rb').read()).hexdigest()

def sc_name(s_in):
    return [k for k, v in SC.PAT.items() if v == s_in][0]

def duipai(hist):
    st = SC.init()
    sts, log = M.snn_run(P1, hist)
    bad = 0
    for k, s in enumerate(hist):
        s_in, teach = M.parse(s)
        st, ev = SC.step(st, sc_name(s_in), teach=teach)
        my = sts[k + 1]
        if ([[st['w'][i][j] for j in range(2)] for i in range(4)] != [[my['w'][(i, j)] for j in range(2)] for i in range(4)]
                or st['v'] != my['v'] or st['x'] != my['x'] or st['y'] != my['y']
                or any(ev['comp'][ij] != log[k]['comp'][ij] for ij in ev['comp'])):
            bad += 1
    return bad

badr = sum(duipai(h) for h in rhist)

def by_proc(host, states, sigs, T, eps):
    """逐过程的有效差异见证（只组合〔十七〕附录 A.1 的 avail_at、comp_of、hc_status，判值规则同 hdyn_cert）：
    返回 {过程: (拍, 目标, A 支, D 支)}，每一过程取窗内第一条见证。"""
    got = {}
    for k, s in enumerate(sigs):
        w = states[k]
        U = JH.avail_at(host, states, k, T, eps)
        if not U:
            continue
        y = host.act(w, s)
        for (p, x, _c) in host.comps(w, y, s):
            if x not in T or p in got:
                continue
            c = JH.comp_of(host, w, y, s, p, x)
            if abs(c) <= eps:
                continue
            st_, wt = JH.hc_status(host, w, s, p, x, U, eps)
            if st_ == '有效差异已获证':
                got[p] = (k, x, wt[2], wt[3])
    return got

def relax_regimes(rho, Lmax):
    pr = M.params(RELAX=rho)
    Hr = M.SNNJH(pr)
    out = []
    for L in range(Lmax):
        st = M.snn_run(pr, FORM + ['rest'] * L)[0][-1]
        z = min(M.z_of(pr, st, (0, 0)), M.z_of(pr, st, (1, 0)))
        _a, la = M.snn_run(pr, WIN, st)
        e = dict(st)
        e['w'] = {k: pr['W0'] for k in st['w']}
        _b, lb = M.snn_run(pr, WIN, e)
        stj = Hr.from_st(st)
        g = by_proc(Hr, JH.run(Hr, stj, WIN), WIN, ['w00', 'w10'], EPS)
        pa_ = JH.part(Hr, stj, ['w00', 'w10'], ['A'], EPS)
        out.append(dict(L=L, z=z, s1=sum(x['g'][0] for x in la), s0=sum(x['g'][0] for x in lb), wit=g, part=pa_))
    return out

reg = relax_regimes(F(1, 16), 60)
LP = next(o['L'] for o in reg if o['s1'] == o['s0'])
LR = next(o['L'] for o in reg if o['z'] <= EPS)
seg1 = all(o['s1'] != o['s0'] and 'PLUS' in o['wit'] and o['wit']['PLUS'][3] == 0 for o in reg if o['L'] < LP)
seg2 = all(o['s1'] == o['s0'] and not o['wit'] and o['part'] is not None and o['part'][1] == 'vt0' for o in reg if LP <= o['L'] < LR)
full_ok, maxc = True, F(0)
prr = M.params(RELAX=F(1, 16))
for L in range(LP, LR):
    st_ = M.snn_run(prr, FORM + ['rest'] * L)[0][-1]
    _s, lg_ = M.snn_run(prr, WIN, st_)
    for ev_ in lg_:
        for ij in ((0, 0), (1, 0)):
            for c_ in ev_['comp'][ij][:3]:
                maxc = max(maxc, abs(c_))
                full_ok &= abs(c_) <= EPS
zs_ = [min(M.z_of(prr, M.snn_run(prr, FORM + ['rest'] * L)[0][-1], ij) for ij in ((0, 0), (1, 0))) for L in range(LP, LR + 1)]
mono_ = all(zs_[k + 1] < zs_[k] for k in range(len(zs_) - 1))

# s2 同拍竞争：去向的历史依赖
P3 = M.params(WTA=True, GRESET=True)
def wta(zs, h):
    st = M.snn_init(P3)
    for k, v in zs.items():
        st['w'][k] += v
    sts, log = M.snn_run(P3, h, st)
    return sts[-1], sum(e['g'][0] for e in log), sum(e['g'][1] for e in log)
hA = ['A', 'rest'] * 30
r0 = wta({(0, 0): F(1, 100)}, hA)
r1_ = wta({(0, 1): F(1, 100)}, hA)
rs = wta({}, hA)
~~~

# 定理 20-4（二′）启动条件：(A, 静) 协议下 v 的上界 a0/(1 − λ²)

_sts, lgx = M.snn_run(P3, ['A', 'rest'] * 40, sx)

thr = P3['THETA'] * (1 - P3['LEAK'] ** 2)
below, above = [], []
for a0 in (thr - F(1, 64), thr, thr + F(1, 64), thr + F(1, 8)):
    w_ = {(i, j): P3['W0'] for i in range(4) for j in range(2)}

    (above if a0 > thr else below).append((fs(a0), first))

      (nofire, fs(F(1, 100) / (1 - P3['LEAK'] ** 2)), fs(thr), below, above),

    for a1 in (thr - F(1, 64), thr + F(1, 64), F(1, 2)):

never1 = not any(e['g'][1] for e in lgc)

for a_ in (F(1, 2), F(3, 4)):

check('C1v′ 定理 20-4（二′）的分报：无竞争、无别路复位的积分支（s1 律）上逐输出「输出 j 终会发放 ⟺ a_j > θ(1 − λ²)」在 12 组 (a0, a1) 上成立 %s；竞争支（WTA + 全体复位）上反例 X20-2：a0 = 1、a1 = 1/2 > 7/16（满足 C_win）输出 1 永不发放 %s——竞争下只签整体启动（max a_j > θ(1 − λ²)）与首发胜者；严格平局 a0 = a1 时首发两输出同发 %s' %
      (po_ok, never1, ties), po_ok and never1 and all(t == (1, 1) for t in ties))

def gd_err_after(eta, n=40):
    H = M.GDJH(eta=eta)
    st = dict(H.init)
    for _ in range(n):
        st = H.step(st, 'A:t10')
    return abs(1 - H.act(st, 'A')['y0'])

def all_comps_T(H, states, sigs, T):
    """完整清单检验：窗内每拍每一过程在 T 各位上的分项（按拍前态重算）。返回 (最大绝对值, 非零阈下个数, 越阈个数)。"""
    mx, sub, eff = F(0), 0, 0
    for k, s_ in enumerate(sigs):
        y_ = H.act(states[k], s_)
        for (p_, x_, c_) in H.comps(states[k], y_, s_):
            if x_ in T:
                mx = max(mx, abs(c_))
                sub += 1 if 0 < abs(c_) <= EPS else 0
                eff += 1 if abs(c_) > EPS else 0
    return mx, sub, eff

def j18_gd(inside, n_beats=4):
    """从原 GD（单过程）的真实轨迹编译：输出 0 的闭合投影 {z00, z10}（其写入只读这两位与信号；证明见正文 4.2.6（五））。
    每拍 t = 0…n−1 呈现 A:t10：SGD_zi0@t = η(t₀ − y0) = (η − 2ηW0) − η(z00 + z10)，D12 线性律；拍 0 即含此过程（首拍误差项）。
    目标以律的偏置进入；B 内另有纯读取过程 FWD（读 z00、z10，零分项）承担 Part。window = 拍 1…n−1，拍 0 为窗前形成拍。"""
    sites = ('z00', 'z10', 'y0')
    eta = F(1, 4)
    ev, links = [], []
    for t in range(n_beats):
        for x in ('z00', 'z10'):
            nm = 'SGD_%s@%d' % (x, t)
            ev.append({'类': '过程', '名': nm, '拍': t, '目标': x, '载体在内': inside, '律': 'linear', '读取': ('z00', 'z10'),
                       '系数': -eta, '偏置': eta - 2 * eta * W0g, 'cert_eff': 1, 'cert_read': 1})
            if t >= 1:      # 拍 0 尚无已形成位，无历史路径可连（上游求值器只接受窗内状态结点）
                links += [('state:z00:%d' % t, nm, 1), ('state:z10:%d' % t, nm, 1)]
        nm = 'FWD@%d' % t
        ev.append({'类': '过程', '名': nm, '拍': t, '目标': 'y0', '载体在内': True, '律': 'linear', '读取': ('z00', 'z10'),
                   '系数': F(0), '偏置': F(0), 'cert_eff': 1, 'cert_read': 1})
        if t >= 1:
            links += [('state:z00:%d' % t, nm, 1), ('state:z10:%d' % t, nm, 1)]
    jr = JX.j18_check(sites, (F(0),) * 3, tuple(range(1, n_beats)), EPS, ('z00', 'z10'), ev, links, closed_graph=True)
    return jr, ev, links

(js, evs, lks), (jn, evn, lkn) = j18_gd(True), j18_gd(False)
# 独立比对：以原机制 GD（jhc 接口）逐拍逐位对照 F 轨迹（不比较同一手编表的两套执行）
gd_traj = JH.run(G, G.init, ['A:t10'] * 4)
f_ok = all(F(js['F 轨迹'][k]['z%s0' % i]) == gd_traj[k + 2]['w%s0' % i] - W0g for k in range(3) for i in '01')
pre_ok = F(js['原机制轨迹'][0]['z00']) == gd_traj[2]['w00'] - W0g and gd_traj[1]['w00'] - W0g == F(1, 8)
# 合法对照：在各拍把 z00 设回 0，F 律值与原 GD 过程分项逐一相同
con_ok = True
for k in range(1, 4):
    stc = dict(gd_traj[k], w00=W0g)
    law_v = (F(1, 4) - 2 * F(1, 4) * W0g) - F(1, 4) * ((stc['w00'] - W0g) + (stc['w10'] - W0g))
    con_ok &= law_v == JH.total_comp(G, stc, 'A:t10', 'SGD', 'w00')
# 投影闭合：改输出 1 的权重不改输出 0 位的分项
st_c = dict(gd_traj[2], w01=F(7, 8), w11=F(-3, 8))
closed = all(JH.total_comp(G, st_c, 'A:t10', 'SGD', x) == JH.total_comp(G, gd_traj[2], 'A:t10', 'SGD', x) for x in ('w00', 'w10'))

def inner_loop(jr, ev, links, B_sites=('z00', 'z10', 'y0')):
    """〔封一〕第七节，从事件表与求值结果独立核：存在 Act_F = 1 且 HistCond_F = 1 的事件，其载体在 B 内，
    且其证据路径的每一起点都是 B 内结构位的状态结点。"""
    carrier = {e['名']: e['载体在内'] for e in ev if e['类'] == '过程'}
    srcs = {}
    for a, b, c in links:
        srcs.setdefault(b, []).append(a)
    for e in jr['事件']:
        if e['Act_F'] == 1 and e['HistCond_F'] == 1 and carrier.get(e['id']):
            if all(a.startswith('state:') and a.split(':')[1] in B_sites for a in srcs.get(e['id'], [])):
                return 1
    return 0

ils, iln = inner_loop(js, evs, lks), inner_loop(jn, evn, lkn)

# 第七步：去向的历史无关（满秩）与零空间永隐（缺秩）

def cycle_matrix(pats, eta):
    n = 4
    Mx = [[F(int(i == j)) for j in range(n)] for i in range(n)]
    for pn in pats:
        s = M.PAT[pn]
        P = [[F(int(i == j)) - eta * s[i] * s[j] for j in range(n)] for i in range(n)]
        Mx = JX.mat_mul(P, Mx)
    return Mx

def inf_norm(A):
    return max(sum(abs(v) for v in row) for row in A)

Mfull = cycle_matrix(['A', 'B', 'A2', 'C'], F(1, 4))
Mk = Mfull
k_c = 1
while inf_norm(Mk) >= 1 and k_c < 64:
    Mk = JX.mat_mul(Mk, Mfull)
    k_c += 1
check('C2k 第七步（满秩字母 {A, B, A2, C}，η = 1/4）：一周期映射线性部分 M 的 %d 次幂 ∞-范数 %s < 1——周期映射为压缩，不动点唯一、全局吸引：去向与既有迹应无关（定理 20-2（一）的模型证书）' %
      (k_c, fl(inf_norm(Mk))), inf_norm(Mk) < 1)
Mdef = cycle_matrix(['A', 'B', 'A2', 'AB'], F(1, 4))
nul = [F(1), F(-1), F(1), F(-1)]
fixed = all(sum(Mdef[i][j] * nul[j] for j in range(4)) == nul[i] for i in range(4))
Acl = Mdef
Cread = [list(map(F, M.PAT[s])) for s in ('A', 'B', 'A2', 'AB')]
hid_tr = JX.hidden_forever_lti(Acl, Cread, nul)
hid_c = JX.hidden_forever_lti(Acl, [list(map(F, M.PAT['C']))], nul)

      kvres[1][0] == '未获证' and kvres[2][0] == '1' and all(u[1] == '1' for u in kvres[2][1][6]) and kvres['L2'][0] == '未获证')
K = M.KVJH(N=6, layers=1)
wk = JH.run(K, K.init, ['p'])[-1]
dil = []
for k in range(5):
    y0 = K.readout(wk, F(1))
    w2 = dict(wk, k1_0=F(0), v1_0=F(0))
    dil.append(abs(y0 - K.readout(w2, F(1))))
    wk = K.step(wk, 'q')
check('C3c KV 参与度稀释：旧条目（p）载体值不变，此后每追加一条 q，其对探针 1 的参与效应依次为 %s——单元素零变化，势层退浅' %
      [fs(v) for v in dil], all(dil[k + 1] < dil[k] for k in range(4)) and wk['k1_0'] == F(1))
fw = {}
for Hh in (M.FWHebbJH(), M.FWDeltaJH(), M.FWHebbJH(lam=F(1))):
    h = ['a', 'b', 'a', 'c', 'a']
    S = JH.run(Hh, Hh.init, h)
    T = [x for x in Hh.X if abs(S[-1][x]) > EPS]
    fw[Hh.name + ('1' if getattr(Hh, 'lam', None) == 1 else '')] = JH.hdyn_cert(Hh, S, h, T, EPS)
check('C3d 快权重：外积写入（线性注意）的证书 %s，见证过程 %s，A 支 %s、D 支 %s（写入不读史，历史条件只由衰减直读承担，同「晒热的石头」型）；delta 规则写入证书 %s，见证 %s，A 支 %s、D 支 %s（经检索活动读史，活动中介）；λ = 1（无衰减）时外积证书 %s——直读承担随之消失' %
      (fw['FWHebb'][0], fw['FWHebb'][1][2], fs(fw['FWHebb'][1][-3]), fs(fw['FWHebb'][1][-2]),
       fw['FWDelta'][0], fw['FWDelta'][1][2], fs(fw['FWDelta'][1][-3]), fs(fw['FWDelta'][1][-2]), fw['FWHebb1'][0]),
      fw['FWHebb'][1][2] == 'DECAY' and fw['FWHebb'][1][-3] == 0 and fw['FWDelta'][1][2] == 'DELTA' and fw['FWDelta'][1][-2] == 0 and fw['FWHebb1'][0] == '未获证')
Hd, Hh_ = M.FWDeltaJH(), M.FWHebbJH(lam=F(1))
rd = {}
for Hx in (Hd, Hh_):
    s0 = JH.run(Hx, Hx.init, ['a'])[-1]
    s1 = JH.run(Hx, Hx.init, ['a', 'c'])[-1]
    rd[Hx.name] = (Hx.act(s0, 'a')['r0'], Hx.act(s0, 'a')['r1'], Hx.act(s1, 'a')['r0'], Hx.act(s1, 'a')['r1'])
check('C3e 快权重交叠（键 a = (1, 0)、c = (1, 1) 交叠于第一维，值 a → (1, 0)、c → (0, 1) 冲突）：delta 规则（β = 1/2）写入 c 后 a 的检索由 (%s, %s) 变为 (%s, %s)（交叠上新覆旧）；外积（λ = 1）由 (%s, %s) 变为 (%s, %s)（串扰叠加，旧分量保留）' %
      tuple(fs(v) for v in rd['FWDelta'] + rd['FWHebb']),
      rd['FWDelta'][2] < rd['FWDelta'][0] and rd['FWHebb'][2] == rd['FWHebb'][0] and rd['FWHebb'][3] > rd['FWHebb'][1])
rngf = random.Random(20261108)
fbad = 0
for _ in range(200):
    Sm = [[F(rngf.randint(-4, 4), 4) for _b in range(2)] for _a in range(2)]
    ka, kc = (F(1), F(0)), (F(1), F(rngf.randint(0, 2)))
    vc = (F(rngf.randint(-2, 2), 2), F(rngf.randint(-2, 2), 2))
    beta, lam = F(1, 2), F(rngf.randint(1, 4), 4)
    Sk = lambda S_, k_: [S_[a][0] * k_[0] + S_[a][1] * k_[1] for a in range(2)]
    kk = kc[0] * ka[0] + kc[1] * ka[1]
    skc = Sk(Sm, kc)
    Sd = [[Sm[a][b] + beta * (vc[a] - skc[a]) * kc[b] for b in range(2)] for a in range(2)]
    Sh = [[lam * Sm[a][b] + vc[a] * kc[b] for b in range(2)] for a in range(2)]
    dd_ = [Sk(Sd, ka)[a] - Sk(Sm, ka)[a] for a in range(2)]
    dh_ = [Sk(Sh, ka)[a] - Sk(Sm, ka)[a] for a in range(2)]
    fbad += sum(1 for a in range(2) if dd_[a] != beta * kk * (vc[a] - skc[a]))
    fbad += sum(1 for a in range(2) if dh_[a] != (lam - 1) * Sk(Sm, ka)[a] + kk * vc[a])
check('C3f P20-7 的检索差闭式（200 个随机有理态，种子 20261108）：delta 规则 ΔS k_a = β(k_c·k_a)(v_c − S k_c)；外积 ΔS k_a = (λ − 1)S k_a + (k_c·k_a)v_c；违例 %d——旧值分量的符号由投影 k_c·k_a、v_c − S k_c 在旧值方向的分量与衰减共同决定，不由「交叠且值冲突」单独决定' % fbad, fbad == 0)
# ============================================================ C4
print('=' * 60)
print('C4　跨宿载条件定理与预测（模型内数值）')
# P20-1 承重观测：同一新位 w20 的单拍实际增量；两臂只差旧位 w10（W0 对 W0 + d），共同输入 A2，
# 同一残留 (v, x, y)、同一新位初值；SNN 无教学，delta 侧带同一目标 t0 = 1；重合激活 a_old = a_new = 1。
def snn_inc(pp, v0, x1, x2, y0, w20, w10):
    st_ = M.snn_init(pp)
    st_['v'] = [v0, F(0)]
    st_['x'] = [F(0), x1, x2, F(0)]
    st_['y'] = [y0, F(0)]
    st_['w'][(2, 0)] = w20
    st_['w'][(1, 0)] = w10
    _n, ev_ = M.snn_beat(pp, st_, 'A2')
    return ev_['comp'][(2, 0)][3], ev_['g'][0], ev_['comp'][(2, 0)]

n_dom = n_flip = n_clamp = bad_f = bad_s = 0
min_flip = None
for rho in (F(0), F(1, 16), F(1, 8)):
    pp = M.params(RELAX=rho)
    for v0 in (F(0), F(1, 4), F(1, 2), F(3, 4)):
        for x1 in (F(0), F(1)):
            for x2 in (F(0), F(1, 2), F(1), F(3, 2)):
                for y0 in (F(0), F(1, 2), F(1), F(3, 2)):
                    for w20 in (F(0), F(1, 64), F(1, 8), F(1, 4), F(1, 2), F(1), F(3, 2), F(2)):
                        for dd in (F(1, 32), F(1, 8), F(1, 4), F(1, 2), F(3, 4), F(1)):
                            i1, g1, c1 = snn_inc(pp, v0, x1, x2, y0, w20, pp['W0'] + dd)
                            i0, g0_, c0 = snn_inc(pp, v0, x1, x2, y0, w20, pp['W0'])
                            chi = i1 - i0
                            if chi < 0:
                                bad_s += 1
                            raw1, raw0 = sum(c1[:3]), sum(c0[:3])
                            unclamped = 0 <= w20 + raw1 <= pp['W_MAX'] and 0 <= w20 + raw0 <= pp['W_MAX']
                            if unclamped:
                                n_dom += 1
                                if chi != (pp['A_PLUS'] * (x2 * pp['DEC'] + 1) - pp['A_MINUS']) * (g1 - g0_):
                                    bad_f += 1
                                if g1 > g0_:
                                    n_flip += 1
                                    min_flip = chi if min_flip is None else min(min_flip, chi)
                            else:
                                n_clamp += 1
check('C4a 承重观测 P20-1（定理 20-3′）：同一新位 w20 的单拍实际增量之差 χ（旧位有迹应 − 擦除；共同输入 A2，同一残留与新位初值，无教学，ρ ∈ {0, 1/16, 1/8}）。无夹限域 %d 例中 χ = (A₊x′₂ − A₋)Δg 违例 %d，门翻转 %d 例 χ 最小 %s ≥ A₊ − A₋ = 1/16；含夹限 %d 例在内 χ < 0 违例 %d（夹限单调，符号保留、裕度不签）' %
      (n_dom, bad_f, n_flip, fs(min_flip), n_clamp, bad_s), bad_f == 0 and bad_s == 0 and n_flip > 0 and min_flip >= F(1, 16))
nd_bad, nd = 0, 0
for dd in (F(1, 32), F(1, 8), F(1, 4), F(1, 2), F(1)):
    for w20 in (F(-1), F(0), F(1, 4), F(1)):
        for v10 in (F(0), F(1, 4)):
            stA_ = dict(G.init, w20=w20, w00=v10)
            stB_ = dict(stA_, w10=W0g + dd)
            stA_['w10'] = W0g
            ia = G.step(stB_, 'A2:t10')['w20'] - w20
            ib = G.step(stA_, 'A2:t10')['w20'] - w20
            nd += 1
            nd_bad += (ia - ib) != -F(1, 4) * dd
p0 = M.snn_init(M.S1)
p0['w'][(1, 0)] = F(3, 4)
_n, evp = M.snn_beat(M.S1, p0, 'A2')
_n, eve = M.snn_beat(M.S1, M.snn_init(M.S1), 'A2')
~~~

check('C4a′ delta 侧同一观测：χ = −η a\_new a\_old d\_old，%d 例违例 %d（与目标、新位初值无关）。分离：申报 d\_old ≥ d\_min = 1/8 时 delta 侧 χ ≤ −1/32，SNN 侧 χ ≥ 0，读数误差 e \< 1/64 时两侧读数集不交，否则未判。只读 PLUS 分项另一读数：反例 X20-6（静息残留、w10 = 3/4）PLUS 差 %s、实际增量差 %s——两读数不同，承重取实际增量' %  
&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(nd, nd\_bad, fs(evp\['comp'\]\[(2, 0)\]\[0\] - eve\['comp'\]\[(2, 0)\]\[0\]), fs(evp\['comp'\]\[(2, 0)\]\[3\] - eve\['comp'\]\[(2, 0)\]\[3\])),  
&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nd\_bad == 0 and evp\['comp'\]\[(2, 0)\]\[0\] - eve\['comp'\]\[(2, 0)\]\[0\] == F(1, 8) and evp\['comp'\]\[(2, 0)\]\[3\] - eve\['comp'\]\[(2, 0)\]\[3\] == F(1, 16))  
dz\_snn = z20w - z20e  
dz\_gd = (SA2\[-1\]\['w20'\] - W0g) - (SA2e\[-1\]\['w20'\] - W0g)  
check('C4a″ 多拍模型值（不承重，只作模型值与条件预测 P20-1（丙））：A 形成后 (A2，静，静) 多次呈现，新位窗末获得量之差——SNN %s，GD %s' %

      (fl(dz_snn), fl(dz_gd)), dz_snn > 0 and dz_gd < 0)
# 反例 X20-10：两臂步长不同（各有下界 1/8）时 delta 侧 χ 可落入候选甲侧——D5 须要求每对共享同一实际步长
def gd_inc(eta, w10):
    H_ = M.GDJH(eta=eta)
    st_ = dict(H_.init, w10=w10)
    return H_.step(st_, 'A2:t10')['w20'] - st_['w20']
c0x, c1x = gd_inc(F(1, 8), W0g), gd_inc(F(1, 4), W0g + F(1, 8))
shared_ok = all(gd_inc(e_, W0g + F(1, 8)) - gd_inc(e_, W0g) <= -e_ * F(1, 8) for e_ in (F(1, 8), F(1, 4), F(3, 8)))
~~~

check('C4h 反例 X20-10：σ⁰ 臂 η₀ = 1/8、σ¹ 臂 η₁ = 1/4（同有下界 1/8），新位增量 %s 与 %s，χ = %s \> 0 落入候选甲侧；两臂共享同一 η ∈ {1/8, 1/4, 3/8} 时 χ ≤ −η·d %s——承重前件 D5 须为「每对共享同一实际步长」' %

      (fs(c0x), fs(c1x), fs(c1x - c0x), shared_ok), c1x - c0x == F(1, 32) and shared_ok)
# 单拍定理 20-3 的有限核验：随机状态
rng2 = random.Random(20261106)
bad = {'Hebb': 0, 'LMS': 0, 'STDP': 0}
for _ in range(400):
    a_new, a_old = F(1), F(1)
    w_new = F(rng2.randint(0, 32), 32)
    w_old = F(rng2.randint(1, 32), 32)
    base_ = F(rng2.randint(0, 32), 32)
    t = F(rng2.randint(0, 64), 32)
    eta = F(1, 8)
    y_w = base_ + w_new * a_new + w_old * a_old
    y_e = base_ + w_new * a_new
    if not (eta * a_new * y_w > eta * a_new * y_e):
        bad['Hebb'] += 1
    if not (eta * a_new * (t - y_w) < eta * a_new * (t - y_e)):
        bad['LMS'] += 1
    th = F(rng2.randint(1, 64), 32)
    if not ((1 if y_w >= th else 0) >= (1 if y_e >= th else 0)):
        bad['STDP'] += 1
check('C4b 定理 20-3 单拍形式的有限核验（400 个随机有理状态，种子 20261106）：c_new = η·a_new·e(y)，旧迹应使 y 增大时，赫布 e = y 交叉项 > 0、误差 e = t − y 交叉项 < 0、阈门控 e = [y ≥ θ] 交叉项 ≥ 0；违例 %s' % bad,
      sum(bad.values()) == 0)
# P20-2 撤外标

      (fl(zW[0]), fl(zW[1]), SGu[-1] == SG[-1]), zW[1] > zW[0] and SGu[-1] == SG[-1])
# P20-4 遗忘 = 交叠投影

sA2 = M.PAT['A2']
pred = {}
for tgt, nm in (('01', 'A2'), ('01', 'B')):
    s = M.PAT[nm]
    ycur = sum(thA[M.wname(i, 0)] * s[i] for i in range(4))
    nrm = sum(v * v for v in s)
    dlt = {M.wname(i, 0): s[i] * (F(int(tgt[0])) - ycur) / nrm for i in range(4)}
    predA = sum((thA[M.wname(i, 0)] + dlt[M.wname(i, 0)]) * M.PAT['A'][i] for i in range(4))
    stx = dict(thA)
    for _ in range(60):
        stx = G.step(stx, '%s:t%s' % (nm, tgt))
    pred[nm] = (predA, G.act(stx, 'A')['y0'])
check('C4d P20-4 遗忘 = 交叠投影（定理 20-2（三））：任务 A 已精确拟合后训练 A2 → 目标 0，预言 A 的输出 0 = %s，60 拍 SGD 后 %s；训练 B（与 A 不交叠）预言 %s，实跑 %s（无遗忘）' %
      (fs(pred['A2'][0]), fl(pred['A2'][1]), fs(pred['B'][0]), fs(pred['B'][1])),

Xr = [M.PAT['A2'], M.PAT['A2']]
gram = [[sum(a * b for a, b in zip(r1, r2)) for r2 in Xr] for r1 in Xr]
det0 = gram[0][0] * gram[1][1] - gram[0][1] * gram[1][0] == 0
stp = dict(thA)

      (det0, fs(pred['A2'][0]), fl(G.act(stp, 'A')['y0'])), det0 and abs(G.act(stp, 'A')['y0'] - pred['A2'][0]) < F(1, 10 ** 6))
s1A2_A = M.snn_run(P2, ['A', 'rest', 'rest'], s_with[-1])[1]

      (sum(e['g'][0] for e in s1A2_A), fs(dz00), fl(M.z_of(P2, s_with[-1], (1, 0)) - M.z_of(P2, stF, (1, 0)))),

# P20-5 的推论：沉默迹应的「节省」——[L_P, L_Ret) 内以紧邻两次 A 为强提示，有迹应侧发放而擦除侧不发放的区间
prs = M.params(RELAX=F(1, 16))
sav = []
for L in range(REG[0], REG[1]):
    st = M.snn_run(prs, FORM + ['rest'] * L)[0][-1]
    _a, la_ = M.snn_run(prs, ['A', 'A'], st)
    e_ = dict(st)
    e_['w'] = {k: prs['W0'] for k in st['w']}
    _b, lb_ = M.snn_run(prs, ['A', 'A'], e_)
    sav.append((L, sum(x['g'][0] for x in la_), sum(x['g'][0] for x in lb_)))
LS = max([L for (L, a_, b_) in sav if a_ > b_] or [REG[0] - 1]) + 1

check('C4g P20-5 推论（沉默迹应与节省；ρ = 1/16）：L ∈ \[%d, %d) 内单次 A 的发放读数已不分两侧，而紧邻两次 A 的强提示下有迹应侧发放、擦除侧不发放，直到 L\_S = %d；\[%d, %d) 内强提示亦不分（z\_T 仍 \> ε）' %

      (REG[0], LS, LS, LS, REG[1]), REG[0] < LS < REG[1] and all(a_ > b_ for (L, a_, b_) in sav if L < LS))
# ============================================================ C5
print('=' * 60)
print('C5　与生物侧（第十九篇）的对应')
rng3 = random.Random(20261107)
bad_up, bad_dn, n_ok = 0, 0, 0
for _ in range(300):
    d1 = {M.wname(i, j): F(rng3.randint(0, 24), 16) for i in range(4) for j in range(2)}
    bump = F(rng3.randint(1, 8), 16)
    d2 = dict(d1)
    d2['w10'] = d1['w10'] + bump
    r_ = dict(v=[F(rng3.randint(0, 8), 16), F(0)], x=[F(0), F(rng3.randint(0, 4), 4), F(0), F(0)], y=[F(rng3.randint(0, 4), 4), F(0)])
    c1 = sum(c for _, c in HX.comps(d1, HX.read(d1, r_, 'A2'), 'A2')['w20'])
    c2 = sum(c for _, c in HX.comps(d2, HX.read(d2, r_, 'A2'), 'A2')['w20'])
    if c2 < c1:
        bad_up += 1
    g1 = sum(c for _, c in GX.comps(d1, GX.read(d1, None, 'A2:t10'), 'A2:t10')['w20'])
    g2 = sum(c for _, c in GX.comps(d2, GX.read(d2, None, 'A2:t10'), 'A2:t10')['w20'])
    if g2 >= g1:
        bad_dn += 1
    n_ok += 1

# 改写命运（定理 20-7）
e_ = {(0, 0): F(-1, 8), (1, 0): F(1, 8)}
def snn_pair(hist):
    a_ = dict(stA, w=dict(stA['w']))
    for k, v in e_.items():
        a_['w'][k] += v
    sa, la = M.snn_run(P1, hist, a_)
    sb, lb = M.snn_run(P1, hist, stA)
    gates_same = [x['g'] for x in la] == [x['g'] for x in lb]
    return {k: sa[-1]['w'][k] - sb[-1]['w'][k] for k in ((0, 0), (1, 0), (2, 0))}, gates_same

def gd_pair(hist, base):
    a_ = dict(base, w00=base['w00'] - F(1, 8), w10=base['w10'] + F(1, 8))
    ra, rb = JH.run(G, a_, hist)[-1], JH.run(G, base, hist)[-1]
    return [ra['w%d0' % i] - rb['w%d0' % i] for i in range(4)]

gV = gd_pair(['A:t10', 'A2:t10'] * 30, SG[-1])

def no_clamp(log):
    return all(e['comp'][ij][3] == sum(e['comp'][ij][:3]) for e in log for ij in e['comp'])
a_ = dict(stA, w=dict(stA['w']))
a_['w'][(0, 0)] -= F(1, 8)
a_['w'][(1, 0)] += F(1, 8)

rngb = random.Random(20261109)
fb = 0
for _ in range(400):
    pp = M.params(RELAX=F(rngb.randint(0, 2), 16))
    sa_ = M.snn_init(pp)
    sa_['x'] = [F(rngb.randint(0, 3), 2) for _i in range(4)]
    sb_ = dict(sa_, x=list(sa_['x']), w=dict(sa_['w']))
    sa_['y'] = [F(rngb.randint(0, 3), 2), F(0)]
    sb_['y'] = [F(rngb.randint(0, 3), 2), F(0)]
    for ij in sa_['w']:
        sa_['w'][ij] = F(rngb.randint(4, 40), 32)
        sb_['w'][ij] = F(rngb.randint(4, 40), 32)
    sa_['v'] = sb_['v'] = [F(rngb.randint(0, 4), 8), F(0)]
    sig = rngb.choice(['A', 'A2', 'B', 'AB'])
    _x, ea = M.snn_beat(pp, sa_, sig)
    _x, eb = M.snn_beat(pp, sb_, sig)
    s_in = M.PAT[sig]
    for (i, j) in sa_['w']:
        if j != 0:
            continue
        xp = sa_['x'][i] * pp['DEC'] + s_in[i]
        dg = ea['g'][0] - eb['g'][0]
        dy = sa_['y'][0] - sb_['y'][0]
        dw = sa_['w'][(i, 0)] - sb_['w'][(i, 0)]
        pred = pp['A_PLUS'] * xp * dg - pp['A_MINUS'] * s_in[i] * (pp['DEC'] * dy + dg) - pp['RELAX'] * dw
        fb += sum(ea['comp'][(i, 0)][:3]) - sum(eb['comp'][(i, 0)][:3]) != pred

actual2 = sum(la2[1]['comp'][(1, 0)][:3]) - sum(lb2[1]['comp'][(1, 0)][:3])

        od = dict(nr, v=[v0, F(0)])
        _q, ra_ = M.snn_beat(P1, od, 'A')
        _q, rb_ = M.snn_beat(P1, nr, 'A')
        flip_pred = rb_['vt'][0] < P1['THETA'] <= rb_['vt'][0] + P1['LEAK'] * v0
        n_pr += 1
        bad_pr += (ra_['g'][0] != rb_['g'][0]) != flip_pred
nr = M.snn_init(P1)
nr['w'][(0, 0)] = F(3, 4) - F(1, 400)
_q, rv1 = M.snn_beat(P1, dict(nr, v=[F(1, 200), F(0)]), 'A')
_q, rv0 = M.snn_beat(P1, nr, 'A')

Xv = [list(map(F, M.PAT['A'])), list(map(F, M.PAT['A2']))]
Gm = [[sum(Xv[a][i] * Xv[b][i] for i in range(4)) for b in range(2)] for a in range(2)]
dt = Gm[0][0] * Gm[1][1] - Gm[0][1] * Gm[1][0]
Gi = [[Gm[1][1] / dt, -Gm[0][1] / dt], [-Gm[1][0] / dt, Gm[0][0] / dt]]
PV = [[sum(Xv[a][i] * Gi[a][b] * Xv[b][j] for a in range(2) for b in range(2)) for j in range(4)] for i in range(4)]
Mk_, kb, ka = Mm, None, None
for k in range(1, 40):

通过　C8e′ 反例 X20-12（ρ = 1/16、反传关）：首拍势差与翻门保留（vt0 1 对 7/8，发放 1 对 0），PLUS、MINUS 两臂皆 0，但被改位 w10 的直读弛豫写入差 -1/128 ≠ 0；未直接改动的目标位 w20 写入差 0——「切断回写则写入差消失」只对未直接改动的目标位、且须被切断通道承担全部来源  
通过　C8f 改写命运（定理 20-7（一），SNN s1 律、ρ = 0）：同一改写 e = (w00 −1/8, w10 +1/8)。只呈现 A：两臂门序列相同 True ⇒ 改写差逐位原样保留 {(0, 0): '-1/8', (1, 0): '1/8', (2, 0): '0'}；呈现 A2：门序列不同 True，改写被放大并募集新位（w20 差 0.053572，w10 差 0.178641）  
通过　C8g 改写命运（定理 20-7（二），GD 满秩/缺秩）：同一改写 e = (−1/8, +1/8, 0, 0)。继续只训练 A：e ⊥ s\_A，差原样留存 \['-1/8', '1/8', '0', '0'\]；训练 {A, A2}：差在 span(s\_A, s\_A2) 上的分量回拉（60 步后差 → \['-0.083336', '0.083333', '-0.083331', '0.000000'\]，与极限差 \< 10⁻⁴），在其正交补上的分量 (−1/12, 1/12, −1/12, 0) 留存；停训：留存 \['-1/8', '1/8', '0', '0'\]  
通过　C8f′ 改写差的一般式：同输入、同前突触迹时位 (i, 0) 未夹限的实际更新差 = A₊x′Δg − A₋s\_i(DEC·Δy + Δg) − ρΔw；400 个随机态对（种子 20261109，含 Δy ≠ 0、ρ ∈ {0, 1/16, 1/8}）违例 0。反例 X20-3：第二拍 Δg = 1，同残留简式给 1/8，实际 3/32（Δy ≠ 0）；反例 X20-4：同门、两臂同触上夹限，原差 1/64 下一拍为 0。C8f 的 A-only 两臂无有效夹限 True、残留起点相同——保差的前件在该实例上成立  
通过　C8h 可截取性：改写 w01 += 1/64 与不改，在固定时窗 5 拍、输入族 {A, B, A2, 静}⁵ 的全部 1024 条史上门序列相同 1022 条，其中全部 8 位逐拍实际增量相同且改写差原样保留 1022 条；另 2 条输出 1 翻门。等价的只是该窗与输入族上的门序列与写入序列；分级势（vt1）不同，长期可互换不由此推出  
通过　C8i 维护窗（定理 20-8，程序宿载）：（一）被改位 w21 的输入在 (A，静，静) × 3 中不激活——活动逐拍相同 True；（二）隔离所改通道：B 之后输出 0 的积分残留 v0 = 1/2 ≠ 0 时改 w00，下一 A 的输出 0 驱动「保留残留 − 同一改写后权重的清洁起点」= λv0 = 3/8 True；（三）阈下残留：v0 \< ε 也可翻门——反例 X20-5：v0 = 1/200 时驱动 801/800 对 399/400、发放 1 对 0；判据「门不同 ⟺ vt\_clean \< θ ≤ vt\_clean + λv0」（单拍、冻结读数 g0、探针 A）在 96 例上违例 0：阈下等门须残留传播量 λv0 小于门裕度，与残留是否小于 ε 无关

通过　C8j 反例 X20-14（改前对改后，同一残留与输入）：s1 冻结可塑、探针 A、残留全为 0 True，w00 = 5/8、w10 = 1/4，w00 增 1/8：输出 0 驱动 7/8 → 1，g0 0 → 1——残留清空不保证改前改后同门；改前改后同活动只由定理 20-8（一）（被改位窗内始终不读）给出  
通过　C8j′ 反例 X20-15（同一改后权重，保留残留对清空残留）：只改入口 2 的 w20（探针 A 不激活入口 2），v0 = 1/4：清洁驱动 7/8、保留残留驱动 17/16，g0 0 → 1；定理 20-8（三）判据 vt\_clean \< θ ≤ vt\_clean + λv0 给出翻门 True——「被改位不在读取集」不用于此对照
